<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">

<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{78}{6} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times 3 \times 13}{2 \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel{2} \times \cancel{3} \times 13}{\cancel{2} \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
&= 13
\end{aligned}
$

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$78 \div 6 = 13$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Write down two numbers that add to $14$ and multiply to $48$. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Write down two numbers that add to &#36;14&#36; and multiply to &#36;48&#36;. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the first number $= a$

Let the second number $= b$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a + b      &= 14 \\\\
a \times b &= 48
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Check the possible values</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

ABBR: We can find the answer using trial and error.


The two numbers $a$ and $b$ must satisfy both of these conditions:

- Their sum is $14$.
- Their product is $48$.

Factor pairs of $48 = (1, 48), (2, 24), (3, 16), (4, 12), (6, 8)$

The table below checks the possible factor pairs.


| Factor pair $(a, b)$ |    $a + b$    | $= 14$? |
| ------------------ | ----------- | ----- |
| $(1, 48)$            | $1 + 48 = 49$ |   No    |
| $(2, 24)$            | $2 + 24 = 26$ |   No    |
| $(3, 16)$            | $3 + 16 = 19$ |   No    |
| $(4, 12)$            | $4 + 12 = 16$ |   No    |
| $(6, 8)$             | $6 + 8 = 14$  |   Yes   |


</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The two numbers are $6$ and $8$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out the following: $21 - 5 \times 2 + 6$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out the following: &#36;21 - 5 \times 2 + 6&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: Using BIDMAS

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 21 - 5 \times 2 + 6 \\\\
&= 21 - 10 + 6 \\\\
&= 11 + 6 \\\\
&= 17
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$17$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Alison, Bethany and Catherine are three sisters. 
They are $6$ years old, $7$ years old and $12$ years old. 

Bethany is older than Alison. 
Catherine's age is a prime number. 

What is each girl's age?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Alison, Bethany and Catherine are three sisters. 
They are &#36;6&#36; years old, &#36;7&#36; years old and &#36;12&#36; years old. 

Bethany is older than Alison. 
Catherine's age is a prime number. 

What is each girl's age?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Only $7$ is a prime number, so Catherine is $7 \ \text{years}$ old.

The remaining ages are $6$ and $12$. Bethany is older than Alison, so Bethany is $12 \ \text{years}$ old.

Alison is $6 \ \text{years}$ old.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Alison $=$ $6 \ \text{years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Bethany $=$ $12 \ \text{years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Catherine $=$ $7 \ \text{years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Only &#36;7&#36; is a prime number, so Catherine is &#36;7 \ \text{years}&#36; old.

The remaining ages are &#36;6&#36; and &#36;12&#36;. Bethany is older than Alison, so Bethany is &#36;12 \ \text{years}&#36; old.

Alison is &#36;6 \ \text{years}&#36; old.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;6 \ \text{years}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question-nav">

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

math.time.clock
</span>
</li>
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
frame.draw

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Draw the hour and minute hands on this clock to show the time $22{:}30$.

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school/2017/paper-1/questions/s01-q10-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

What is the reflex angle between the two hands on this clock?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Draw the hour and minute hands on this clock to show the time &#36;22{:}30&#36;.

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;

What is the reflex angle between the two hands on this clock?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: The minute hand of a clock moves by $6 \degree$ every minute.

</div>
<div class="tip">

ABBR: The hour hand of a clock moves by $0.5 \degree$ every minute.

</div>
<div class="tip">

ABBR: The hour hand of a clock moves by $30 \degree$ every hour.

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Draw the clock</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/missing/missing_image.svg" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="working" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Reflex angle</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

On an analogue clock, $22{:}30$ is the same as $10{:}30$.

On an accurate clock, find the angle of each hand from $12$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Minute hand</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Minutes past the hour} &= 30 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text{Angle from }12        &= 30 \times 6 \\\\
                            &= 180 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Hour hand</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Angle at }10{:}00 &= 10 \times 30 \\\\
                        &= 300 \degree \\\\
\text{Extra angle in }30\text{ minutes} &= 30 \times 0.5 \\\\
                        &= 15 \degree \\\\
\text{Angle from }12    &= 300 + 15 \\\\
                        &= 315 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Smaller angle} &= 315 - 180 \\\\
                     &= 135 \degree \\\\
\text{Reflex angle}  &= 360 - 135 \\\\
                     &= 225 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$225 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_note_md_clock_minute_hand_every_minute
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_note_md_clock_hour_hand_every_minute
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_note_md_clock_hour_hand_every_hour
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title="Draw the clock"&gt;

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="working" kind="essential" /&gt;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title="Reflex angle"&gt;

On an analogue clock, &#36;22{:}30&#36; is the same as &#36;10{:}30&#36;.

On an accurate clock, find the angle of each hand from &#36;12&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Minute hand"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Minutes past the hour} &= 30 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text{Angle from }12        &= 30 \times 6 \\\\
                            &= 180 \degree
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Hour hand"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Angle at }10{:}00 &= 10 \times 30 \\\\
                        &= 300 \degree \\\\
\text{Extra angle in }30\text{ minutes} &= 30 \times 0.5 \\\\
                        &= 15 \degree \\\\
\text{Angle from }12    &= 300 + 15 \\\\
                        &= 315 \degree
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Smaller angle} &= 315 - 180 \\\\
                     &= 135 \degree \\\\
\text{Reflex angle}  &= 360 - 135 \\\\
                     &= 225 \degree
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;225 \degree&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

The results for Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Thursday are shown in the pictogram. Monday

|-------|---------------------------------|
|Tuesday  |$\spadesuit \spadesuit$            |
|Wednesday|$\spadesuit \spadesuit \dfrac{1}{2}\spadesuit$ |

|Friday   |                                   |

**Question**

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">

<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
frame.labelled
</span>

<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
marker.find-complement
</span>
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

From Year $10$ all pupils must join the Combined Cadet Force ("CCF") or opt for School Community Service 
("SCS"). Pupils can not opt for both CCF and SCS.

Rachel asked $150$ Year $9$ pupils whether they wanted to opt for CCF or SCS next year.

$60\%$ of the pupils said that they wanted to opt for CCF.

$\dfrac{1}{6}$ of the pupils said that they wanted to opt for SCS.

The rest of the pupils said that they hadn't yet decided.

How many pupils hadn't yet decided whether to do CCF or carry out SCS next year?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">From Year &#36;10&#36; all pupils must join the Combined Cadet Force ("CCF") or opt for School Community Service 
("SCS"). Pupils can not opt for both CCF and SCS.

Rachel asked &#36;150&#36; Year &#36;9&#36; pupils whether they wanted to opt for CCF or SCS next year.

&#36;60\%&#36; of the pupils said that they wanted to opt for CCF.

&#36;\dfrac{1}{6}&#36; of the pupils said that they wanted to opt for SCS.

The rest of the pupils said that they hadn't yet decided.

How many pupils hadn't yet decided whether to do CCF or carry out SCS next year?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Calculate groups</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{CCF pupils}       &= 60\% \text{ of } 150 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{60}{100} \times 150 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{60 \times 150}{100} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{6\cancel{0} \times 15\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} \\\\
                        &= 6 \times 15 \\\\
                        &= 90 \\\\
\text{SCS pupils}       &= \dfrac{1}{6} \times 150 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{150}{6} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{6 \times 25}{6} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{\cancel{6} \times 25}{\cancel{6}} \\\\
                        &= 25 \\\\
\text{Undecided pupils} &= 150 - 90 - 25 \\\\
                        &= 35
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$35$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Calculate groups"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{CCF pupils}       &= 60\% \text{ of } 150 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{60}{100} \times 150 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{60 \times 150}{100} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{6\cancel{0} \times 15\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}} \\\\
                        &= 6 \times 15 \\\\
                        &= 90 \\\\
\text{SCS pupils}       &= \dfrac{1}{6} \times 150 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{150}{6} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{6 \times 25}{6} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{\cancel{6} \times 25}{\cancel{6}} \\\\
                        &= 25 \\\\
\text{Undecided pupils} &= 150 - 90 - 25 \\\\
                        &= 35
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Sam thinks of a number.

He multiplies that number by $5$. 

Then he subtracts $12$. 

Then he divides by $3$. 

Finally he adds $17$.

His answer is $38$.

What number did Sam originally think of?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Sam thinks of a number.

He multiplies that number by &#36;5&#36;. 

Then he subtracts &#36;12&#36;. 

Then he divides by &#36;3&#36;. 

Finally he adds &#36;17&#36;.

His answer is &#36;38&#36;.

What number did Sam originally think of?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the number $= a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac{5a - 12}{3} + 17              &= 38 \\\\
\dfrac{5a - 12}{3}                   &= 38 - 17 \\\\
\dfrac{5a - 12}{3}                   &= 21 \\\\
5a - 12                              &= 21 \times 3 \\\\
5a - 12                              &= 63 \\\\
5a                                   &= 63 + 12 \\\\
5a                                   &= 75 \\\\
a                                    &= \dfrac{5 \times 15}{5} \\\\
a                                    &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 15}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
a                                    &= 15
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number $= 15$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$15$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let the number &#36;= a&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Equation"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac{5a - 12}{3} + 17              &= 38 \\\\
\dfrac{5a - 12}{3}                   &= 38 - 17 \\\\
\dfrac{5a - 12}{3}                   &= 21 \\\\
5a - 12                              &= 21 \times 3 \\\\
5a - 12                              &= 63 \\\\
5a                                   &= 63 + 12 \\\\
5a                                   &= 75 \\\\
a                                    &= \dfrac{5 \times 15}{5} \\\\
a                                    &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 15}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
a                                    &= 15
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Number &#36;= 15&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">

<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
marker.interpret-remainder
</span>
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A bus can carry $52$ passengers. How many buses will be needed to transport $993$ people to a sports day?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A bus can carry &#36;52&#36; passengers. How many buses will be needed to transport &#36;993&#36; people to a sports day?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

A remainder means another bus is needed for the people left over.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Calculation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Buses needed} &= \dfrac{993}{52} \\\\
                    &= 19 \ \text{remainder} \ 5 \\\\
                    &= 20
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

A remainder means another bus is needed for the people left over.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Calculation"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Buses needed} &= \dfrac{993}{52} \\\\
                    &= 19 \ \text{remainder} \ 5 \\\\
                    &= 20
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Anila wants to buy a pair of jeans in Kentucky. They are priced at $\text{\textdollar}72$ before allowing for the sales tax. How much does Anila pay for the pair of jeans?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Anila wants to buy a pair of jeans in Kentucky. They are priced at &#36;\text{\textdollar}72&#36; before allowing for the sales tax. How much does Anila pay for the pair of jeans?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

If the price is increased by $6 \%$, then the new price will be $100 + 6 = 106 \%$ of the original price.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Sale price</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}  &= 106 \% \text{ of } 72 \\\\
                   &= \dfrac{106}{100} \times 72 \\\\
                   &= \dfrac{106 \times 72}{100} \\\\
                   &= \dfrac{7632}{100} \\\\
                   &= \text{\textdollar}76.32
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text{\textdollar}76.32$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

If the price is increased by &#36;6 \%&#36;, then the new price will be &#36;100 + 6 = 106 \%&#36; of the original price.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Sale price"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}  &= 106 \% \text{ of } 72 \\\\
                   &= \dfrac{106}{100} \times 72 \\\\
                   &= \dfrac{106 \times 72}{100} \\\\
                   &= \dfrac{7632}{100} \\\\
                   &= \text{\textdollar}76.32
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: -0.4rem 0 1rem;">

inherits parent tags: yes

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Peter buys a bag of sweets in Kentucky. He pays $\text{\textdollar}4.77$ including sales tax. 
What was the price of sweets before sales tax was added?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Peter buys a bag of sweets in Kentucky. He pays &#36;\text{\textdollar}4.77&#36; including sales tax. 
What was the price of sweets before sales tax was added?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the price before sales tax $= a$

If the price is increased by $6 \%$, then the price including sales tax will be $100 + 6 = 106 \%$ of the original price.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
106 \% \text{ of } a  &= 4.77 \\\\
\dfrac{106}{100} \times a &= 4.77 \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac{4.77 \times 100}{106} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac{477 \times 100}{106 \times 100} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac{9 \times 53 \times 50 \times 2}{2 \times 53 \times 100} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac{9 \times \cancel{53} \times 50 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2} \times \cancel{53} \times 100} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac{9 \times 50}{100} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac{450}{100} \\\\
a                         &= \text{\textdollar}4.50
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Price before sales tax $= \text{\textdollar}4.50$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text{\textdollar}4.50$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let the price before sales tax &#36;= a&#36;

If the price is increased by &#36;6 \%&#36;, then the price including sales tax will be &#36;100 + 6 = 106 \%&#36; of the original price.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Equation"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
106 \% \text{ of } a  &= 4.77 \\\\
\dfrac{106}{100} \times a &= 4.77 \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac{4.77 \times 100}{106} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac{477 \times 100}{106 \times 100} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac{9 \times 53 \times 50 \times 2}{2 \times 53 \times 100} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac{9 \times \cancel{53} \times 50 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2} \times \cancel{53} \times 100} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac{9 \times 50}{100} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac{450}{100} \\\\
a                         &= \text{\textdollar}4.50
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Price before sales tax &#36;= \text{\textdollar}4.50&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

</div>

</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;\text{\textdollar}4.50&#36;

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A vowel?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A vowel?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Probability}  = \dfrac { \text{ Favourable outcomes} } { \text{Total outcomes} }$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

The vowels are $\text{A}$, $\text{A}$, $\text{E}$ and $\text{E}$, so there are $4$ vowels.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{P(vowel)} &= \dfrac{2 + 2}{12} \\\\
                &= \dfrac{4}{12} \\\\
                &= \dfrac{4}{3 \times 4} \\\\
                &= \dfrac{\cancel{4}}{3 \times \cancel{4}} \\\\
                &= \dfrac{1}{3}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac{1}{3}$

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How long does it take the sails to turn through a right angle?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How long does it take the sails to turn through a right angle?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

A right angle is one quarter of a full turn.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken} &= \dfrac{40}{4} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac{10 \times 4}{4} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac{10 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4}} \\\\
                  &= 10 \ \text{seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$10 \ \text{seconds}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A right angle is one quarter of a full turn.

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken} &= \dfrac{40}{4} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac{10 \times 4}{4} \\\\
                  &= \dfrac{10 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4}} \\\\
                  &= 10 \ \text{seconds}
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many turns do the sails make in fifty-six minutes?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many turns do the sails make in fifty-six minutes?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of turns} &= \dfrac{\text{Total time}}{\text{Time for 1 turn}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac{56 \ \text{minutes}}{40 \ \text{seconds}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac{56 \times 60 \ \text{seconds}}{40 \ \text{seconds}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac{56 \times 60}{40} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac{2 \times 28 \times 20 \times 3}{2 \times 20} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac{\cancel{2} \times 28 \times \cancel{20} \times 3}{\cancel{2} \times \cancel{20}} \\\\
                       &= 28 \times 3 \\\\
                       &= 84
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$84$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of turns} &= \dfrac{\text{Total time}}{\text{Time for 1 turn}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac{56 \ \text{minutes}}{40 \ \text{seconds}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac{56 \times 60 \ \text{seconds}}{40 \ \text{seconds}} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac{56 \times 60}{40} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac{2 \times 28 \times 20 \times 3}{2 \times 20} \\\\
                       &= \dfrac{\cancel{2} \times 28 \times \cancel{20} \times 3}{\cancel{2} \times \cancel{20}} \\\\
                       &= 28 \times 3 \\\\
                       &= 84
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

marker.make-units-consistent

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Stuart's patio is $5 \ \text{metres}$ long and $5 \ \text{metres}$ wide.

Stuart wants to cover the patio with paving stones. Each paving stone is $50 \ \text{centimetres}$ long and 
$50 \ \text{centimetres}$ wide.

How many paving stones does Stuart need to buy?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Stuart's patio is &#36;5 \ \text{metres}&#36; long and &#36;5 \ \text{metres}&#36; wide.

Stuart wants to cover the patio with paving stones. Each paving stone is &#36;50 \ \text{centimetres}&#36; long and 
&#36;50 \ \text{centimetres}&#36; wide.

How many paving stones does Stuart need to buy?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of stones} &= \dfrac{\text{Area of the patio}}{\text{Area of a stone}} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{5 \ \text{m} \times 5 \ \text{m}}{50 \ \text{cm} \times 50 \ \text{cm}} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{5 \times 100 \ \text{cm} \times 5 \times 100 \ \text{cm}}{50 \ \text{cm} \times 50 \ \text{cm}} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{5 \times 100 \times 5 \times 100}{50 \times 50} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{5 \times 10 \times 10 \times 5 \times 10 \times 10}{5 \times 10 \times 5 \times 10} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 1 \cancel{0} \times 10 \times \cancel{5} \times 1 \cancel{0} \times 10}{\cancel{5} \times 1 \cancel{0} \times \cancel{5} \times 1 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                         &= 10 \times 10 \\\\
                         &= 100
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$100$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_note_md_unit_no_dot
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_rectangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of stones} &= \dfrac{\text{Area of the patio}}{\text{Area of a stone}} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{5 \ \text{m} \times 5 \ \text{m}}{50 \ \text{cm} \times 50 \ \text{cm}} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{5 \times 100 \ \text{cm} \times 5 \times 100 \ \text{cm}}{50 \ \text{cm} \times 50 \ \text{cm}} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{5 \times 100 \times 5 \times 100}{50 \times 50} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{5 \times 10 \times 10 \times 5 \times 10 \times 10}{5 \times 10 \times 5 \times 10} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 1 \cancel{0} \times 10 \times \cancel{5} \times 1 \cancel{0} \times 10}{\cancel{5} \times 1 \cancel{0} \times \cancel{5} \times 1 \cancel{0}} \\\\
                         &= 10 \times 10 \\\\
                         &= 100
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;100&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion" id="question-the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school-11-plus-maths-2017-paper-1-1-22-1">

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

marker.make-units-consistent
</span>

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">

</div>
<div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: -0.4rem 0 1rem;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #15803d; border-radius: 999px; background: #dcfce7; color: #14532d; padding: 0.24rem 0.7rem; font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 700;">
inherits parent tags: yes
</span>
</div>
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Stuart has $\pounds 200$ left after buying the paving stones. Stuart wants to put a picket fence around **three sides** of his patio.

Each panel of picket fencing is $1 \ \text{metre}$ long and costs $\pounds 10.50$. 
Stuart will also have to pay a delivery charge of $\pounds 5$ regardless of the number of panels of picket fencing he purchases.

How much money does Stuart have left after buying the picket fencing?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Stuart has &#36;\pounds 200&#36; left after buying the paving stones. Stuart wants to put a picket fence around **three sides** of his patio.

Each panel of picket fencing is &#36;1 \ \text{metre}&#36; long and costs &#36;\pounds 10.50&#36;. 
Stuart will also have to pay a delivery charge of &#36;\pounds 5&#36; regardless of the number of panels of picket fencing he purchases.

How much money does Stuart have left after buying the picket fencing?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fence length}   &= 3 \times 5 \\\\
                      &= 15 \ \text{m} \\\\
\text{Panels needed}  &= \dfrac{15 \ \text{m}}{1 \ \text{m}} \\\\
                      &= 15 \\\\
\text{Cost of panels} &= 15 \times 10.50 \\\\
                      &= \pounds 157.50 \\\\
\text{Money left}     &= 200 - 157.50 - 5 \\\\
                      &= \pounds 37.50
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 37.50$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fence length}   &= 3 \times 5 \\\\
                      &= 15 \ \text{m} \\\\
\text{Panels needed}  &= \dfrac{15 \ \text{m}}{1 \ \text{m}} \\\\
                      &= 15 \\\\
\text{Cost of panels} &= 15 \times 10.50 \\\\
                      &= \pounds 157.50 \\\\
\text{Money left}     &= 200 - 157.50 - 5 \\\\
                      &= \pounds 37.50
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;\pounds 37.50&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Elizabeth writes down:

One multiple of $13$ and two different factors of $77$.

Elizabeth adds up her three numbers. Her answer is greater than fifty but less than sixty.

What three numbers could Elizabeth have written down?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Elizabeth writes down:

One multiple of &#36;13&#36; and two different factors of &#36;77&#36;.

Elizabeth adds up her three numbers. Her answer is greater than fifty but less than sixty.

What three numbers could Elizabeth have written down?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Factor pairs of $77$ are $(1, 77)$ and $(7, 11)$.

Since the total must be less than $60$, $77$ cannot be one of the chosen numbers.

The possible factors of $77$ are $1$, $7$ and $11$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Multiples of 13</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

ABBR: We can find the answer using trial and error.


The first number must be a multiple of $13$. The other two numbers must be different factors of $77$.


| Multiple of $13$ | Factors of $77$ | Total                 | Result  |
|----------------|---------------|---------------------|-------|
| $13 \times 1 = 13$ | $1, 7$        | $13 + 1 + 7 = 21$     | Too low |
| $13 \times 1 = 13$ | $1, 11$       | $13 + 1 + 11 = 25$    | Too low |
| $13 \times 1 = 13$ | $7, 11$       | $13 + 7 + 11 = 31$    | Too low |
| $13 \times 2 = 26$ | $1, 7$        | $26 + 1 + 7 = 34$     | Too low |
| $13 \times 2 = 26$ | $1, 11$       | $26 + 1 + 11 = 38$    | Too low |
| $13 \times 2 = 26$ | $7, 11$       | $26 + 7 + 11 = 44$    | Too low |
| $13 \times 3 = 39$ | $1, 7$        | $39 + 1 + 7 = 47$     | Too low |
| $13 \times 3 = 39$ | $1, 11$       | $39 + 1 + 11 = 51$    | Works   |
| $13 \times 3 = 39$ | $7, 11$       | $39 + 7 + 11 = 57$    | Works   |
| $13 \times 4 = 52$ | $1, 7$        | $52 + 1 + 7 = 60$     | Too high |


</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Elizabeth could have written down $1$, $11$ and $39$, or $7$, $11$ and $39$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$(1, 11, 39)$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$(7, 11, 39)$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Factor pairs of &#36;77&#36; are &#36;(1, 77)&#36; and &#36;(7, 11)&#36;.

Since the total must be less than &#36;60&#36;, &#36;77&#36; cannot be one of the chosen numbers.

The possible factors of &#36;77&#36; are &#36;1&#36;, &#36;7&#36; and &#36;11&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="verify" title="Multiples of 13"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_note_md_trial_and_error_find_answer

The first number must be a multiple of &#36;13&#36;. The other two numbers must be different factors of &#36;77&#36;.

&lt;PaperTable&gt;

| Multiple of &#36;13&#36; | Factors of &#36;77&#36; | Total                 | Result  |
|----------------|---------------|---------------------|-------|
| &#36;13 \times 1 = 13&#36; | &#36;1, 7&#36;        | &#36;13 + 1 + 7 = 21&#36;     | Too low |
| &#36;13 \times 1 = 13&#36; | &#36;1, 11&#36;       | &#36;13 + 1 + 11 = 25&#36;    | Too low |
| &#36;13 \times 1 = 13&#36; | &#36;7, 11&#36;       | &#36;13 + 7 + 11 = 31&#36;    | Too low |
| &#36;13 \times 2 = 26&#36; | &#36;1, 7&#36;        | &#36;26 + 1 + 7 = 34&#36;     | Too low |
| &#36;13 \times 2 = 26&#36; | &#36;1, 11&#36;       | &#36;26 + 1 + 11 = 38&#36;    | Too low |
| &#36;13 \times 2 = 26&#36; | &#36;7, 11&#36;       | &#36;26 + 7 + 11 = 44&#36;    | Too low |
| &#36;13 \times 3 = 39&#36; | &#36;1, 7&#36;        | &#36;39 + 1 + 7 = 47&#36;     | Too low |
| &#36;13 \times 3 = 39&#36; | &#36;1, 11&#36;       | &#36;39 + 1 + 11 = 51&#36;    | Works   |
| &#36;13 \times 3 = 39&#36; | &#36;7, 11&#36;       | &#36;39 + 7 + 11 = 57&#36;    | Works   |
| &#36;13 \times 4 = 52&#36; | &#36;1, 7&#36;        | &#36;52 + 1 + 7 = 60&#36;     | Too high |

&lt;/PaperTable&gt;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Elizabeth could have written down &#36;1&#36;, &#36;11&#36; and &#36;39&#36;, or &#36;7&#36;, &#36;11&#36; and &#36;39&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A cube has side lengths of $5 \ \text{cm}$. What is the volume of the cube? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A cube has side lengths of &#36;5 \ \text{cm}&#36;. What is the volume of the cube? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Volume} &= 5 \times 5 \times 5 \\\\
              &= 125 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$125 \ \text{cm}^3$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A second cube has a volume of $27 \ \text{cm}^3$. 

What is the total surface area of the second cube?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A second cube has a volume of &#36;27 \ \text{cm}^3&#36;. 

What is the total surface area of the second cube?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$

</div>
<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Surface area of cube} = 6 \times \text{edge} \times \text{edge}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Side length}   &= \sqrt[3]{27} \\\\
                     &= \sqrt[3]{3 \times 3 \times 3} \\\\
                     &= 3 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Surface area} &= 6 \times 3^2 \\\\ 
                    &= 6 \times 9 \\\\ 
                    &= 54 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$54 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_formula_katex_volume_cube
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_formula_katex_surface_area_cube
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;

                     &= \sqrt[3]{3 \times 3 \times 3} \\\\
                     &= 3 \ \text{cm} \\\\

                    &= 6 \times 9 \\\\ 
                    &= 54 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion" id="question-the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school-11-plus-maths-2017-paper-1-1-25-1">

inherits parent tags: yes
</span>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Rinesh started painting his house two years ago. During that year, Rinesh painted one-third of his house.

Last year, Rinesh painted another five-twelfths of his house.

What fraction of his house does Rinesh need to paint this year, in order to finish completely painting his house?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Rinesh started painting his house two years ago. During that year, Rinesh painted one-third of his house.

Last year, Rinesh painted another five-twelfths of his house.

What fraction of his house does Rinesh need to paint this year, in order to finish completely painting his house?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction painted so far} &= \dfrac{1}{3} + \dfrac{5}{12} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{1 \times 4}{3 \times 4} + \dfrac{5}{12} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{4}{12} + \dfrac{5}{12} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{9}{12} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{3 \times 3}{3 \times 4} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 3}{\cancel{3} \times 4} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{3}{4} \\\\
\text{Fraction needed}         &= 1 - \dfrac{3}{4} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{4}{4} - \dfrac{3}{4} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{1}{4} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac{1}{4}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fraction painted so far} &= \dfrac{1}{3} + \dfrac{5}{12} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{1 \times 4}{3 \times 4} + \dfrac{5}{12} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{4}{12} + \dfrac{5}{12} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{9}{12} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{3 \times 3}{3 \times 4} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{\cancel{3} \times 3}{\cancel{3} \times 4} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{3}{4} \\\\
\text{Fraction needed}         &= 1 - \dfrac{3}{4} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{4}{4} - \dfrac{3}{4} \\\\
                               &= \dfrac{1}{4} \\\\
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;\dfrac{1}{4}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
math.number.fraction
</span>
</li>
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
frame.labelled
</span>
