</div>
<div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: -0.4rem 0 1rem;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #15803d; border-radius: 999px; background: #dcfce7; color: #14532d; padding: 0.24rem 0.7rem; font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 700;">
inherits parent tags: yes
</span>
</div>
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Bijal started weeding her garden two weeks ago. During that week, she weeded one-fifth of her garden.

Last week, Bijal weeded two and a half times as much of her garden as she weeded two weeks ago.

What fraction of her garden does Bijal need to weed this week, in order to finish completely weeding her garden?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Bijal started weeding her garden two weeks ago. During that week, she weeded one-fifth of her garden.

Last week, Bijal weeded two and a half times as much of her garden as she weeded two weeks ago.

What fraction of her garden does Bijal need to weed this week, in order to finish completely weeding her garden?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Weeded two weeks ago} &= \dfrac{1}{5} \\\\
\text{Weeded last week}     &= 2.5 \times \dfrac{1}{5} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{5}{2} \times \dfrac{1}{5} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 1}{2 \times \cancel{5}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{1}{2} \\\\
\text{Total weeded}         &= \dfrac{1}{5} + \dfrac{1}{2} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{2}{5 \times 2} + \dfrac{5}{2 \times 5} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{2}{10} + \dfrac{5}{10} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{7}{10} \\\\
\text{Fraction needed}      &= 1 - \dfrac{7}{10} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{10}{10} - \dfrac{7}{10} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{3}{10} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac{3}{10}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Weeded two weeks ago} &= \dfrac{1}{5} \\\\
\text{Weeded last week}     &= 2.5 \times \dfrac{1}{5} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{5}{2} \times \dfrac{1}{5} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{\cancel{5} \times 1}{2 \times \cancel{5}} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{1}{2} \\\\
\text{Total weeded}         &= \dfrac{1}{5} + \dfrac{1}{2} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{2}{5 \times 2} + \dfrac{5}{2 \times 5} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{2}{10} + \dfrac{5}{10} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{7}{10} \\\\
\text{Fraction needed}      &= 1 - \dfrac{7}{10} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{10}{10} - \dfrac{7}{10} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac{3}{10} \\\\
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;\dfrac{3}{10}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Nina is painting garden gnomes. She has four-fifths of a litre of paint. Each garden gnome needs one-twentieth of a litre of paint. How many garden gnomes can she paint?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Nina is painting garden gnomes. She has four-fifths of a litre of paint. Each garden gnome needs one-twentieth of a litre of paint. How many garden gnomes can she paint?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: Dividing fractions is the same as multiplying by the reciprocal (inverse) of the second fraction.

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of gnomes} &= \dfrac{4}{5} \div \dfrac{1}{20} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{4}{5} \times \dfrac{20}{1} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{4 \times 20}{5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{4 \times 4 \times 5}{5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{4 \times 4 \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
                        &= 16
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$16$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_note_md_divide_by_fraction
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of gnomes} &= \dfrac{4}{5} \div \dfrac{1}{20} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{4}{5} \times \dfrac{20}{1} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{4 \times 20}{5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{4 \times 4 \times 5}{5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{4 \times 4 \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
                        &= 16
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Tom has arranged to meet Nic at St. Peters Street in St. Albans at $8{:}30 \ \text{am}$.

Tom lives in Rickmansworth and is planning to travel by bus. Tom thinks that it is a Hertfordshire school holiday and arrives at the bus stop at Rickmansworth just in time to catch the bus. Unfortunately, it is a Hertfordshire school day.

How late does Tom arrive for his meeting with Nic?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Tom has arranged to meet Nic at St. Peters Street in St. Albans at &#36;8{:}30 \ \text{am}&#36;.

Tom lives in Rickmansworth and is planning to travel by bus. Tom thinks that it is a Hertfordshire school holiday and arrives at the bus stop at Rickmansworth just in time to catch the bus. Unfortunately, it is a Hertfordshire school day.

How late does Tom arrive for his meeting with Nic?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Read timetable</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Tom expected to catch the $06{:}55$ HNsch bus at Rickmansworth. On a school day, the next Hsch bus reaches St. Albans, St Peter's Street at $08{:}50$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Late by</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Late by} &= 08{:}50 - 08{:}30 \\\\
               &= 20 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

One angle of an isosceles triangle is $80 \degree$. 

What are the other angles? 

There are two possible solutions to this question.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">One angle of an isosceles triangle is &#36;80 \degree&#36;. 

What are the other angles? 

There are two possible solutions to this question.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

There are two possible cases.

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Case 1</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The $80 \degree$ angle could be the angle that is different.

Let the two equal angles of the isosceles triangle $= a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Case 1 equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a  + a + 80 &= 180 \\\\
2a + 80     &= 180 \\\\
2a          &= 180 - 80 \\\\
2a          &= 100 \\\\
a           &= \dfrac{100}{2} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac{50 \times 2}{2} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac{50 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
a           &= 50 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Case 1 answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The other two angles are $50 \degree$ and $50 \degree$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Case 2</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The $80 \degree$ angle could be one of the two equal angles.

Let the two equal angles of the isosceles triangle $= 80 \degree$

Let the third angle $= b$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Case 2 equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
b  + 80 + 80 &= 180 \\\\
b + 160      &= 180 \\\\
b            &= 180 - 160 \\\\
b            &= 20 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Case 2 answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The other two angles are $80 \degree$ and $20 \degree$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$50 \degree, 50 \degree$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \degree, 80 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_formula_katex_angles_of_triangle
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

</div>

<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the area of this triangle?

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/the-haberdashers-askes-boys-school/2017/paper-1/questions/s01-q28-s01-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the area of this triangle?

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Area of triangle} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times \text{base} \times \text{height}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

In the diagram,


$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area} &= \dfrac{1}{2} \times 5 \times 4 \\\\
            &= \dfrac{5 \times 4}{2} \\\\
            &= \dfrac{5 \times 2 \times 2}{2} \\\\
            &= \dfrac{5 \times 2 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
            &= 5 \times 2 \\\\
            &= 10 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$10 \ \text{cm}^2$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_formula_katex_area_triangle_expanded
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_diagram

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Area} &= \dfrac{1}{2} \times 5 \times 4 \\\\
            &= \dfrac{5 \times 4}{2} \\\\
            &= \dfrac{5 \times 2 \times 2}{2} \\\\
            &= \dfrac{5 \times 2 \times \cancel{2}}{\cancel{2}} \\\\
            &= 5 \times 2 \\\\
            &= 10 \ \text{cm}^2
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;10 \ \text{cm}^2&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

By thinking of two different ways to work out the area of the triangle, calculate the length of arrow.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">By thinking of two different ways to work out the area of the triangle, calculate the length of arrow.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Area of triangle} = \dfrac{1}{2} \times \text{base} \times \text{height}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the length of the arrow $= h$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

In the diagram,


$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac{1}{2} \times 10 \times h &= \dfrac{1}{2} \times 8 \times 6 \\\\
h                              &= \dfrac{8 \times 6 \times 2}{2 \times 10} \\\\
h                              &= \dfrac{8 \times 6 \times \cancel 2}{\cancel 2 \times 10} \\\\
h                              &= \dfrac{48}{10} \\\\
h                               &= 4.8 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Length of the arrow $= 4.8 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4.8 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

There has been a robbery in Alysford. The thieves are planning to go to Habsville Airport to flee the 
country with their loot. The police want to go from Alysford to Habsville Airport by the 
**shortest possible** route. What route should the police take? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">There has been a robbery in Alysford. The thieves are planning to go to Habsville Airport to flee the 
country with their loot. The police want to go from Alysford to Habsville Airport by the 
**shortest possible** route. What route should the police take? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Read map</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The shortest route from Alysford to Habsville Airport is $\text{A} \ \rightarrow \ \text{D} \ \rightarrow \ \text{E} \ \rightarrow \ \text{H}$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Distance</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The distance is $40 + 20 + 30 = 90$ miles.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The police should take route $\text{A} \ \rightarrow \ \text{D} \ \rightarrow \ \text{E} \ \rightarrow \ \text{H}$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text{A} \ \rightarrow \ \text{D} \ \rightarrow \ \text{E} \ \rightarrow \ \text{H}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title="Read map"&gt;

The shortest route from Alysford to Habsville Airport is &#36;\text{A} \ \rightarrow \ \text{D} \ \rightarrow \ \text{E} \ \rightarrow \ \text{H}&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Distance"&gt;

The distance is &#36;40 + 20 + 30 = 90&#36; miles.

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The thieves also want to go from Alysford to Habsville Airport by the **shortest possible** route. However, the thieves also want to make sure that they don't travel on any of the roads being used by the police. What route should the thieves take? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

However, the thieves also want to make sure that they don't travel on any of the roads being used by the police. What route should the thieves take? 
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">

<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Avoid police roads</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The police route uses the roads $\text{A} \ \rightarrow \ \text{D}$, $\text{D} \ \rightarrow \ \text{E}$ and $\text{E} \ \rightarrow \ \text{H}$.

The thieves cannot use these roads.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">

<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Read map</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The shortest remaining route from Alysford to Habsville Airport is $\text{A} \ \rightarrow \ \text{B} \ \rightarrow \ \text{D} \ \rightarrow \ \text{F} \ \rightarrow \ \text{H}$.

The distance is $30 + 20 + 20 + 50 = 120$ miles.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">

<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The thieves should take route $\text{A} \ \rightarrow \ \text{B} \ \rightarrow \ \text{D} \ \rightarrow \ \text{F} \ \rightarrow \ \text{H}$.

</div>

<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$\text{A} \ \rightarrow \ \text{B} \ \rightarrow \ \text{D} \ \rightarrow \ \text{F} \ \rightarrow \ \text{H}$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

For each of the prime numbers in the table below: 

-  Find the remainder when the prime number is divided by $4$; and 
-  Find whether the prime number can be expressed as the sum of two square numbers.

If the prime number can be expressed as the sum of two square numbers, state the two square numbers. If the prime number cannot be expressed as the sum of two square numbers state "Not possible".

Complete the table below. The first two rows have been completed for you.


| Prime  number 	| The remainder when the  prime number is divided by $4$| Can the prime number be expressed as the sum of two square numbers?|
|---	|---	|---	|
| $3$ 	| $3$ 	| Not possible 	|
| $5$ 	| $1$ 	| $1^2 +2^2$ 	|
| $7$ 	|  	|  	|
| $11$ 	|  	|  	|
| $13$ 	|  	|  	|
| $17$ 	|  	|  	|


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">For each of the prime numbers in the table below:

-  Find whether the prime number can be expressed as the sum of two square numbers.

If the prime number can be expressed as the sum of two square numbers, state the two square numbers. If the prime number cannot be expressed as the sum of two square numbers state "Not possible".

Complete the table below. The first two rows have been completed for you.

&lt;PaperTable&gt;

| Prime  number 	| The remainder when the  prime number is divided by &#36;4&#36;| Can the prime number be expressed as the sum of two square numbers?|

| &#36;5&#36; 	| &#36;1&#36; 	| &#36;1^2 +2^2&#36; 	|
| &#36;7&#36; 	|  	|  	|
| &#36;11&#36; 	|  	|  	|
| &#36;13&#36; 	|  	|  	|
| &#36;17&#36; 	|  	|  	|

&lt;/PaperTable&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the link between whether a prime number can be expressed as the sum of two square numbers and the 
remainder when that prime number is divided by $4?$ If you are not sure you can extend the table above and see what happens for other prime numbers.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the link between whether a prime number can be expressed as the sum of two square numbers and the 
remainder when that prime number is divided by &#36;4?&#36; If you are not sure you can extend the table above and see what happens for other prime numbers.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Link</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

A remainder of $1$ when divided by $4$ means that the prime number can be expressed as the sum of two square numbers.

A remainder of $3$ when divided by $4$ means that it cannot.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

A remainder of $1$ means possible. A remainder of $3$ means not possible.

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

A remainder of &#36;3&#36; when divided by &#36;4&#36; means that it cannot.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The first proof that this pattern is true for all prime numbers was claimed by Pierre de Fermat and is called 
Fermat's Christmas Theorem (because Fermat's claim was dated December $25, 1640$).

Circle "YES" for each of the following prime numbers that can be expressed as the sum of two square numbers.

Circle "NO" for each of the following prime numbers that cannot be expressed as the sum of two square numbers.

**You do not need to find the two square numbers**.


| Prime  number 	| Can the prime number be expressed as the sum of two square numbers (YES/NO)? 	|
|---	|---	|
| $58031$ 	|           |
| $58043$ 	|           |
| $58049$ 	|           |
| $58057$ 	|           |


</div>
